<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out $1638 \div 7$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out $1638 \div 7$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1638 \div 7 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1638} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7 \times 234} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{7} \times 234} {\cancel{7}} \\\\
&= 234
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$234$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Divide $31500$ by $30$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Divide $31500$ by $30$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {31500} {30} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1050 \times 3 \times 10} {3 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1050 \times \cancel{3} \times 1\cancel{0}} {\cancel{3} \times 1\cancel{0}} \\\\
&= 1050
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$1050$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>

</code></pre>

<div class="workings">

\end{aligned}

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Calculate $34.3 \div 0.7$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Calculate $34.3 \div 0.7$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{34.3}{0.7} \\\\
&= \dfrac{34.3 \times 10}{0.7 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac{343}{7} \\\\
&= \dfrac{49 \times 7}{7} \\\\
&= \dfrac{49 \times \cancel{7}}{\cancel{7}} \\\\
&= 49
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Divide $5616$ by $8$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Divide $5616$ by $8$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{5616}{8} \\\\
&= \dfrac{702 \times 8}{8} \\\\
&= \dfrac{702 \times \cancel{8}}{\cancel{8}} \\\\
&= 702
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

&= \dfrac{14 \times 5 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{2}} \\\\
&= 14 \times 5 \\\\
&= 70
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

&= \dfrac {3138} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {523 \times 6} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {523 \times \cancel {6}} {\cancel{6}} \\\\

\end{aligned}

**Question**

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>

&= \dfrac{3.6}{0.4} \\\\
&= \dfrac{3.6 \times 10}{0.4 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac{36}{4} \\\\
&= \dfrac{9 \times 4}{4} \\\\
&= \dfrac{9 \times \cancel{4}}{\cancel{4}} \\\\
&= 9
\end{aligned}
$

**Question**

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

\begin{aligned}
&= 3647 \div 7 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3647} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {521 \times 7} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {521 \times \cancel {7} } {\cancel {7} } \\\\
&= 521
\end{aligned}
$

<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$800 \div \ldots = 20$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$800 \div \ldots = 20$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the missing number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
800 \div a               &= 20 \\\\
\dfrac {800} {a}         &= 20 \\\\
a                        &= \dfrac {800} {20} \\\\
a                        &= \dfrac {40 \times 20} {20} \\\\
a                        &= \dfrac {40 \times \cancel {20}} {\cancel{20}} \\\\
a                        &= 40
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$40$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is $423\div9 ?$

- $43$
- $47$
- $35$
- $57$
- $37$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is $423\div9 ?$
<emptyline>
- $43$
- $47$
- $35$
- $57$
- $37$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 423 \div 9  \\\\
&= \dfrac {423} {9} \\\\
&= \dfrac {47 \times 9} {9} \\\\
&= \dfrac {47 \times \cancel{9}} {\cancel {9}} \\\\
&= 47
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A: $6$ 

B: $5$ 

C: $4$ 

D: $3$ 

E: $2$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<emptyline>
A: $6$ 
<emptyline>

<emptyline>
C: $4$ 
<emptyline>
D: $3$ 
<emptyline>
E: $2$
</code></pre>

**Question**

Work out $336 \div  6$

<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

&=  56
\end{aligned}
$

Use your answer to write down the answers to

</div>

&= \dfrac {4000} {40} \\\\

&= \dfrac {\cancel{4} \times 100 \times \cancel{10}} {\cancel{4} \times \cancel{10}} \\\\
&= 100
\end{aligned}
$

</code></pre>

<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
&= 4000 \div 80 \\\\
&= \dfrac {4000} {80} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4000} {40 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {100} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {50 \times 2} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {50 \times \cancel{2}} {\cancel{2}} \\\\
&= 50
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

\begin{aligned}
&= (595 + 595 + 595) \div 17 \\\\
&= \dfrac {595 + 595 + 595} {17} \\\\
&= \dfrac {595 \times 3} {17} \\\\
&= 35 \times 3 \\\\
&= 105
\end{aligned}
$

&= 20
\end{aligned}
$

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is $8000 \div 50?$

&= \dfrac {8000} {50} \\\\
&= \dfrac {8000 \times 2} {50 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {8000 \times 2} {100} \\\\
&= 80 \times 2 \\\\
&= 160
\end{aligned}
$

\begin{aligned}

&= 20
\end{aligned}

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out $4992 \div 16$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out $4992 \div 16$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 4992 \div 16 \\\\
&= \dfrac{4992}{16} \\\\
&= \dfrac{312 \times 4 \times 4}{4 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac{312 \times \cancel{4} \times \cancel{4}}{\cancel{4} \times \cancel{4}} \\\\
&= 312
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Divide $285 \div 3$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Divide $285 \div 3$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 285 \div 3 \\\\
&= \dfrac{285}{3} \\\\
&= \dfrac{95 \times 3}{3} \\\\
&= \dfrac{95 \times \cancel{3}}{\cancel{3}} \\\\
&= 95

**Question**

Divide 301 ÷ 7 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Calculate $391$ divided by seventeen.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Calculate $391$ divided by seventeen.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">

</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{391}{17} \\\\
&= \dfrac{17 \times 23}{17} \\\\
&= \dfrac{\cancel{17} \times 23}{\cancel{17}} \\\\
&= 23
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out $1449 \div 23$

- $65$
- $63$
- $71$
- $76$
- $69$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<emptyline>
- $65$
- $63$
- $71$
- $76$
- $69$
</code></pre>

**Working**

\begin{aligned}
&= 1449 \div 23 \\\\
&= \dfrac{1449}{23} \\\\
&= \dfrac{23 \times 63}{23} \\\\
&= \dfrac{\cancel{23} \times 63}{\cancel{23}} \\\\
&= 63
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

**Question**

$6.05 \div 5$

- $12.1$
- $1.21$
- $21$
- $1.1$
- $1.05$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>

<emptyline>
- $12.1$
- $1.21$
- $21$
- $1.1$
- $1.05$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{6.05}{5} \\\\
&= \dfrac{6.05 \times 100}{5 \times 100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{605}{5 \times 100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{121 \times 5}{5 \times 100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{121 \times \cancel{5}}{\cancel{5} \times 100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{121}{100} \\\\
&= 1.21
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Divide: $78 \div 6$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Divide: $78 \div 6$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 78 \div 6 \\\\
&= \dfrac {78} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {13 \times 6} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {13 \times \cancel{6} } {\cancel{6}} \\\\
&= 13
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Divide $3$ by $0.5$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Divide $3$ by $0.5$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 3 \div 0.5 \\\\
&= \dfrac{3}{0.5} \\\\
&= \dfrac{3 \times 10}{0.5 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac{30}{5} \\\\
&= \dfrac{5 \times 6}{5} \\\\
&= \dfrac{\cancel{5} \times 6}{\cancel{5}} \\\\
&= 6
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Divide $1908$ by $6$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Divide $1908$ by $6$.
</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1908 \div 6 \\\\
&= \dfrac{1908}{6} \\\\
&= \dfrac{318 \times 6}{6} \\\\
&= \dfrac{318 \times \cancel 6}{\cancel 6} \\\\
&= 318
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

Work out $525 \div 25$.

<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 525 \div 25 \\\\
&= \dfrac {525} {25} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 5 \times 21} {5 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{5} \times \cancel{5} \times 21} {\cancel{5} \times \cancel{5}} \\\\
&= 21
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out $6.48 \div 0.6$, writing your answer as a decimal number.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out $6.48 \div 0.6$, writing your answer as a decimal number.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{6.48}{0.6} \\\\
&= \dfrac{6.48 \times 100}{0.6 \times 100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{648}{6 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac{108 \times 6}{6 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac{108 \times \cancel{6}}{\cancel{6} \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac{108}{10} \\\\
&= 10.8
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out $2047 \div 23$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out $2047 \div 23$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 2047 \div 23 \\\\
&= \dfrac{2047}{23} \\\\
&= \dfrac{89 \times 23}{23} \\\\
&= \dfrac{89 \times \cancel{23}}{\cancel{23}} \\\\
&= 89
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out $44.8 \div 0.7$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out $44.8 \div 0.7$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

\begin{aligned}
&= 44.8 \div 0.7 \\\\
&= \dfrac {44.8} {0.7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {44.8 \times 10} {0.7 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac{448} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {64 \times 7} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {64 \times \cancel{7}} {\cancel{7}} \\\\
&= 64
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Calculate $273 \div 7$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Calculate $273 \div 7$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">

</div>

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 273 \div 7 \\\\
&= \dfrac{273}{7} \\\\
&= \dfrac{39 \times 7}{7} \\\\
&= \dfrac{39 \times \cancel 7}{\cancel 7} \\\\
&= 39
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$39$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
&= 273 \div 7 \\\\
&= \dfrac{273}{7} \\\\
&= \dfrac{39 \times 7}{7} \\\\
&= \dfrac{39 \times \cancel 7}{\cancel 7} \\\\
&= 39
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Which **ONE** of the following numbers is NOT divisible by $3?$

- $18171$   

- $44481$   

- $38501$   

- $57060$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>

<emptyline>
- $18171$   
<emptyline>
- $44481$   
<emptyline>
- $38501$   
<emptyline>
- $57060$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: As we know that a number is divisible by 3, if the sum of all its digits is divisible by 3.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

|:------:   |:----------------------------------------------------: | :-----------: |
|  $18171$  | $1 + 8 + 1 + 7 + 1 = \red{18}$                      |  Yes          |
|  $44481$  | $4 + 4 + 4 + 8 + 1 = \red{21}$                      |  Yes          |
|  $38501$  | $3 + 8 + 5 + 0 + 1 = \green{17}$                        |  No           |
|  $57060$  | $5 + 7 + 0 + 6 + 0 = \red{18}$                      |  Yes          |

Number not divisible $= 38501$
</div>
</div>
